| S14 | **Lily:** I'm really **thankful** for my team. Next month, a new **chapter** begins: my own group of sellers! | 林晓：我真的很感激我的团队。下个月，新的篇章就要开始了：我要独立负责一组卖家！ |
| S15 | **Leo:** _No way_(D41)! Congratulations! | Leo：真的假的！恭喜恭喜！ |
| S16 | **Ben:** Nice! _What's been_(D59) the hardest part so far? | Ben：真棒！到现在为止，最难的是什么？ |
| S17 | **Lily:** _To be honest_(D59), phone calls. But I'm less _nervous_(D1) _than before_(D54). | 林晓：说实话，是打电话。不过我没有以前那么紧张了。 |
| S18 | **Joe:** Did you have any fun? | Joe：这个月有没有玩点什么开心的？ |
| S19 | **Lily:** Yes! Grace and I climbed Luofu Mountain at three in the morning. | 林晓：有！吴佳和我凌晨三点去爬了罗浮山。 |
| S20 | **Joe:** Three in the morning? _How come_(D42)? | Joe：凌晨三点？怎么会那么早？ |
| S21 | **Lily:** We wanted to watch the _sunrise_(D57) from the top. I've never seen anything so beautiful. | 林晓：我们想在山顶看日出。我从来没见过那么美的景色。 |
| S22 | **Lily:** My legs hurt for days, but it was _worth it_(D57). | 林晓：我的腿疼了好几天，不过值了。 |

| S26 | Joe smiles. He told Lily the same thing on her first day. | Joe 笑了——林晓第一次来的时候，他也是这么对她说的。 |

## D 原文重点词汇注释

**1. look back on** *phr.* /ˌlʊk ˈbæk ɑːn/ · 位置 S3

- 语义：回顾；回想（过去的一段经历）
- 场景：月底、年底做总结；毕业、离职、告别时回想一段日子
- 搭配：look back on the past month；look back on my first job；When I look back on…, I…
- 例句：When I look back on my first year in Shenzhen, I feel proud. 回想我在深圳的第一年，我感到很自豪。
- 易错：「回顾这个月」不说 ✗ review this month（review 是「复习、审查」）也不说 ✗ look back the month（少了 on）；不带宾语的 look back 是「回头看」：Don't look back!；look back on 后面跟一段经历或一段时间，想念一个人要说 I miss her.（D35），不说 ✗ I look back on Mei.

**2. reflect** *v.* /rɪˈflekt/ · 位置 S4

- 语义：（静下心来）回想、反思
- 场景：总结一段时间、复盘一个项目、一对一谈话之前
- 搭配：take a moment to reflect；reflect on the month；reflect on what went well
- 例句：Take a few minutes to reflect on what went well this week. 花几分钟想一想，这周哪些事做得好。
- 易错：中文「反思」常带「检讨错误」的味道，英文 reflect 是中性的「回头想一想」，好事坏事都想，所以美国经理常说 Let's reflect on what went well.；「反思某事」要加 on：✓ reflect on my month ✗ reflect my month（不加 on 时是「反射、反映」：The lake reflects the mountain.）；重音在后：re-FLECT。

**3. journey** *n.* /ˈdʒɜːrni/ · 位置 S5

- 语义：（一段）历程；旅程
- 场景：讲一段经历、成长、学习过程；比 trip 更有感情
- 搭配：quite a journey；my English journey；a long journey
- 例句：Learning English is a long journey, not a race. 学英语是一段漫长的旅程，不是赛跑。
- 易错：真的出门玩一趟，日常说 trip（D10 business trip、D57 weekend trip）：✗ a weekend journey to Luofu Mountain ✓ a weekend trip；journey 多用来比喻「一段历程」，或指很长的路途；It was quite a journey. 意思是「这一路经历了不少事」，不是「这次旅行很棒」；our 读卷舌的 /ɜːr/，不读「旧泥」。

**4. milestone** *n.* /ˈmaɪlstoʊn/ · 位置 S13

- 语义：里程碑；重要的节点
- 场景：转正、毕业、第一个大客户、搬进自己的房子等人生或项目中重要的一步
- 搭配：a big milestone；reach a milestone；project milestones
- 例句：Our first U.S. client was a big milestone for the team. 第一个美国客户是团队的一个重要里程碑。
- 易错：中文「里程碑」听起来很隆重，英文 milestone 日常也常用：转正、第一次独立打电话都可以说 a big milestone for me；它是可数名词，前面要有 a / the / my：✗ It was big milestone.；项目管理里的 milestones 是「阶段节点」，第三册讲项目进度时还会遇到；mile 读 /maɪl/，结尾的 l 不要读成「尔」。

**5. thankful** *adj.* /ˈθæŋkfl/ · 位置 S14

- 语义：感激的；庆幸的
- 场景：总结、发言时说自己对谁心存感激；也说「幸好……」
- 搭配：thankful for sb / sth；thankful that…；I'm really thankful.
- 例句：I'm thankful for my family's support. 我很感激家人的支持。
- 易错：thankful 是形容词，前面要有 am / is / are：✓ I'm very thankful. ✗ I'm very thank you. ✗ I thankful.；它说的是自己的心情，常用在总结和发言里；当面向一个人道谢还是用 Thank you / Thanks for…（D19）；和 grateful（D43）意思很近，grateful 多用于感谢某个具体的帮助；th 读 /θ/，舌尖放在上下齿之间，不读 /s/。

**6. chapter** *n.* /ˈtʃæptər/ · 位置 S14

- 语义：（人生的）新阶段；（书的）一章
- 场景：换工作、搬到新城市、转正、毕业、开始新的一册书
- 搭配：a new chapter；the next chapter；Chapter 3
- 例句：Moving to Shanghai is a new chapter for Mei. 搬到上海，是小梅人生的新篇章。
- 易错：中文「新篇章」「新阶段」可以直接说 a new chapter，很地道，但别漏 a：✗ start new chapter ✓ start a new chapter / A new chapter begins.；书的「第三章」说 Chapter 3 或 the third chapter；ch 读 /tʃ/（像「吃」），不读 /k/；结尾 ter 美式要卷舌 /tər/。

## E 易混表达检查

### 1. 「了」「过」「刚」「已经」「还没」——have done vs did；just / already / yet（复习 D32、D33、D36、D57）

- 中文想表达：「我转正了」「我吃过」「我刚搬家」「我已经告诉她了」「还没」——中文动词不变，说不说具体时间都是同一个动词。
- 自然英文：I've just passed my probation! / I passed it on Thursday. / I've never seen such a beautiful sunrise. / Have you told Mom yet? — Not yet. / I've already told her.
- 为什么不同：英文要先想清楚两件事：这件事是在说**结果、经历、到现在为止**，还是在说**过去具体的某一次**？只说结果和经历、不提具体时间 → **have done**（I've tried stinky tofu. D32；Shenzhen has changed a lot. D36）；一出现具体的过去时间（yesterday / on Thursday / last year / ago）→ **过去式**（Prices went up last year. D36）。**just / already / yet** 是完成时的三个好帮手（D33）：just「刚」、already「已经」放在 have 和动词中间；yet 放句尾，只用在疑问句和否定句里。美式口语里 just / already 也常配过去式（Grace just got engaged!），但「完成时 + 具体过去时间」永远是错的。今天林晓说 I've just passed my probation!——没提时间，所以用完成时；要是加上 on Thursday，就得改成 I passed it on Thursday.。讲一次旅行也一样（D57）：经过用过去式，经历用 I've never seen…。
- 什么时候用：报喜、说经历、问「……了吗」时用 have done + just / already / yet；一说「哪天、哪年、多久以前」就换成过去式。
- 对比例句：**I've just passed my probation!** 我刚通过试用期！（报喜，不提时间） / **I passed it on Thursday.** 我是周四通过的。（有具体时间） / **Have you ever climbed Luofu Mountain?** — **Yes, I have. I climbed it last weekend.** / **I've already told Mom.** 我已经告诉妈妈了。／**I haven't told Mei yet.** 我还没告诉小梅。 / ✗ I've passed it on Thursday. ✗ I've ever climbed it. ✗ I've told Mei already yesterday.
- 小任务：用英文说：① 我刚搬家了！② 我是上周六搬的。③ 你交房租了吗？——还没。④ 我以前从来没一个人住过。

### 2. 「两年了」「从初中起」「两个月前」——for / since / ago（复习 D31、D34、D36、D58）

- 中文想表达：中文都带着时间，「十年了」「从初中起」「两个月前」，却不在动词上区分「一段时间」「起点」和「过去的一个时间点」。
- 自然英文：We've known each other for ten years. / We've known each other since middle school. / Two months ago, I joined SwiftLink.
- 为什么不同：**for + 一段时间**（ten years / two months / ages），**since + 起点**（middle school / 2020 / I moved here），两个都配 **have done / have been doing**，表示「一直持续到现在」（D31、D34）；**ago** 跟在一段时间**后面**，表示「从现在往回数的那个时间点」，配**过去式**（D36）。中文「我在深圳住了两年了」最容易说成 ✗ I live in Shenzhen two years.——缺了完成时，也缺了 for（D31）。另外三个常见错误：✗ since two years（since 后面要起点）；✗ I've joined SwiftLink two months ago（有 ago 就是具体时间点，要用过去式）；回答 How long have you…? 时，口语里只说 For two years. / Since 2023. 就够了；说完整句子别漏掉 for：I've lived here for two years.（D31）。今天林晓说 We've known each other since middle school（起点），又说 Two months ago, I was just like you（时间点）。
- 什么时候用：问和答「多久了」：How long have you…? + for / since；说「……之前发生了什么」：一段时间 + ago + 过去式。
- 对比例句：**We've known each other for ten years.** 我们认识十年了。 / **We've known each other since middle school.** 我们从初中起就认识。 / **We met ten years ago.** 我们是十年前认识的。 / **I've walked to work since I moved.** ／ **I moved three weeks ago.** / ✗ We've known each other since ten years. ✗ We have met ten years ago.
- 小任务：用英文说：① 我在这家公司工作两个月了。② 我搬家以后，一直走路上班。③ 小梅是上周日去上海的。④ 我三年前搬到深圳。

### 3. 「我正在做饭，停电了」——was doing vs did（复习 D38、D39、D43）

- 中文想表达：「我做饭的时候停电了」「我打电话的时候把包放在座位上」——中文「正在」常常省略，一个「的时候」就把背景和事件都包住了。
- 自然英文：I was cooking dinner when the power went out. / While I was talking on the phone, I put it on the seat. / I was taking a shower. vs I took a shower.
- 为什么不同：讲故事时，英文要把两层分开：**背景**——一个已经在进行的长动作，用 **was / were + -ing**；**事件**——突然发生、打断背景的短动作，用**过去式**（D38）。when 后面常接短动作，while 后面常接长动作（D39）。中国学习者最常见的三个错：① 漏掉 was（✗ I cooking dinner when…），因为中文「我在做饭」里没有和 was 对应的字；② 全用过去式（✗ I cooked dinner when the power went out.——意思变成「停电了我才做饭」）；③ 讲着讲着又滑回现在时（✗ Then my neighbor knocks on the door.）——讲过去的故事，从头到尾都用过去时（D43）。
- 什么时候用：讲一件过去的事、要交代「当时正在干什么」的时候；回答 What were you doing when…?（D38）。
- 对比例句：**I was cooking dinner when the power went out.** 我正在做晚饭，突然停电了。 / **I cooked dinner, and then the power went out.** 我做完了饭，然后停电了。 / **What were you doing at nine last night?** — **I was taking a shower.** / ✗ I cooking dinner when the power went out. ✗ When I was cooking, the power was going out.
- 小任务：用 was / were …ing when… 说：① 我正在开会，妈妈打电话来了。② 我们正在爬山，太阳出来了。

### 4. 「以前」「已经习惯」「慢慢习惯」——used to / be used to / get used to（复习 D52、D53）

- 中文想表达：「我以前每天坐两小时地铁」「我已经习惯一个人住了」「我正在慢慢习惯」——中文用了「以前」「习惯」两个不同的词，英文三个说法却都长得像 used to。
- 自然英文：I used to spend two hours a day on the subway. / I'm used to living alone now. / I'm getting used to it.
- 为什么不同：**used to + 动词原形** = 以前常常（现在不了），说的是过去（D52）；**be used to + 名词 / -ing** = 已经习惯了，说的是现在的状态，这里的 to 是介词（D53）；**get used to + 名词 / -ing** = 慢慢变得习惯，说的是过程（D53）。最常见的错：✗ I'm used to get up early.（be used to 后面要 -ing）；✗ I use to be shy.（肯定句写 used）；否定和疑问反过来写 use：I didn't use to… / Did you use to…?（D52）。一个分辨小窍门：前面有 am / is / are / get 的，后面接 -ing 或名词；前面什么都没有的，后面接动词原形。另外，三个说法读音都是 /ˈjuːstə/，听的时候只能靠前面那个小词判断。
- 什么时候用：讲「以前……，现在……」的变化 → used to；说习惯了、还不习惯 → be used to；说正在适应、会慢慢适应 → get used to。
- 对比例句：**I used to take the subway for an hour.** 我以前每天坐一个小时地铁。（现在不了） / **I'm used to the heat now.** ／ **I'm used to living alone now.** 我已经习惯了。 / **I'm getting used to my new routine.** 我正在慢慢习惯新的作息。 / **I didn't use to drink coffee.** 我以前不喝咖啡。 / ✗ I'm used to live alone. ✗ I use to be shy.
- 小任务：用英文说：① 我以前很害羞。② 我还不习惯早起。③ 别担心，你会慢慢习惯的。

### 5. 问对问题——look like / be like；How come / Why（复习 D39、D42、D56）

- 中文想表达：「她长什么样？」「她是个什么样的人？」中文都是「什么样」；「怎么会……？」和「为什么……？」中文里也差不多。
- 自然英文：What does she look like? / What's she like? / How come you got up at three? / Why did you get up at three?
- 为什么不同：**look like** 只管长相（D39 What does it look like?、D56 I look like my dad）；**be like** 问性格和为人（What's she like? — She's easygoing. D56）。问「她这个人怎么样」，只要不是问外貌，就用 What's she like?。别说 ✗ How does she look like?——how 和 like 只能留一个：What does she look like? 问长相，How does she look? 问「她看起来气色怎么样」。**How come** 是「怎么会、怎么回事」，带着惊讶，后面用**陈述语序**：How come you got up at three?（✗ How come did you get up…?）；**Why** 是中性的「为什么」，后面要倒装：Why did you get up at three?（D42）。How come 多用于口语，正式邮件里写 Why… / Could you explain why…?。今天 Joe 一听「凌晨三点」就说 How come?——一个词就把惊讶带出来了。
- 什么时候用：问长相 → What does … look like?；问性格 → What's … like?；口语里吃惊地问原因 → How come…?；中性地、正式地问原因 → Why…?
- 对比例句：**What does your new manager look like?** — **He's tall, with glasses.**（长相） / **What's your new manager like?** — **He's easygoing.**（性格） / **How come you got up at three?** 你怎么三点就起来了？（吃惊） / **Why did you get up at three?** 你为什么三点起床？（中性） / ✗ How does he look like? ✗ How come did you get up at three?
- 小任务：用英文说：① 朋友说新同事人特别好，你想问「他是个什么样的人？」② 同事今天没来上班，第二天你吃惊地问他「你昨天怎么没来？」

## F 今日核心表达

| 表达 / 句型 | 什么时候用 | 可以替换 | 正式 / 随意 |
|---|---|---|---|
| How long have you…? — For… / Since… | 来自 D31、D34、D58：问和说一件事持续了多久 | lived here / worked at… / been learning English；two years / middle school / I moved here | 正式：I've been with the company since March.｜随意：Two years now. |
| Have you ever…? — Yes, I have. / No, never. I've never… | 来自 D32、D57：问和说经历 | tried stinky tofu / seen the sunrise from a mountain / lived alone | 正式：Have you had a chance to visit our Seattle office?｜随意：Ever tried it? |
| I was …ing when… / While I was…, … | 来自 D38、D39、D43：讲一件过去的事的背景和突发事件 | cooking dinner / the power went out；talking on the phone / I missed my stop | 正式：I was preparing the report when the system went down.｜随意：I was cooking, and all of a sudden, the lights went out! |
| What happened next? / How come…? / No way! / Tell me more! | 来自 D40、D41、D42：追问和回应，让对话接下去 | And then? / Are you kidding? / It turned out… / That's why… | 正式：Could you tell me a little more about that?｜随意：Seriously? Then what? |
| I'm looking for… / Are … included? / Can I exchange it for…? | 来自 D45、D48、D49：在中介、银行、门店说清自己的需求 | a one-bedroom apartment / utilities / a larger size；I'd like to open an account | 正式：I'd like to return this jacket, please. Here's my receipt.｜随意：Can I get a large instead? |
| I used to…, but now… / I'm (not) used to… yet. | 来自 D52、D53、D54：讲过去的习惯和现在的变化 | be really shy / take the subway；living alone / the heat；getting better and better | 正式：In the past, I used to find calls difficult, but they've become easier.｜随意：I used to hate coffee. Not anymore! |
| How's everything going? / To be honest, … / I'm looking forward to + -ing. | 来自 D59：长对话里开场、坦诚地说困难、表达期待 | the new job / phone calls are still hard / taking on more responsibility | 正式：To be honest, I still find phone calls challenging.｜随意：Honestly, calls are still tough. |
| When I look back on…, I'm thankful for… / It was a big milestone. / A new chapter begins. | 来自 D60：总结一段时间、说感受、展望下一步 | this month / my first year；my team / my family；passing probation / moving out | 正式：Looking back on this quarter, I'm thankful for the team's support.｜随意：What a month! Big milestone for me. |

## G 语音与听力

| 词 | 音标 | 音节与重音 | 口型与听感 |
|---|---|---|---|
| milestone | /ˈmaɪlstoʊn/ | 2 个音节 MILE·stone | 重音在前；mile 读 /maɪl/，结尾暗 l 舌尖抵住上齿龈；stone 读 /stoʊn/，o 从「欧」滑到圆唇 |
| journey | /ˈdʒɜːrni/ | 2 个音节 JOUR·ney | our 读卷舌的 /ɜːr/，舌头往后卷、嘴唇微圆；不要读成「旧泥」或 jour-nee 两个一样重的音 |
| reflect | /rɪˈflekt/ | 2 个音节 re·FLECT | 重音在后；re 轻读 /rɪ/；结尾 ct 两个辅音：k 只做口型，t 轻轻收住，不要加「特」 |
| thankful | /ˈθæŋkfl/ | 2 个音节 THANK·ful | th 是 /θ/，舌尖放在上下齿之间送气；a 读 /æ/ 嘴巴张大；ful 弱读成 /fl/，不读 full |
| chapter | /ˈtʃæptər/ | 2 个音节 CHAP·ter | ch 读 /tʃ/，不读 /k/；p 碰到 t 只做口型不爆破；ter 美式卷舌 /tər/ |

- Let's look **back**‿on / the **past** **month**. —— look back on 是短语动词，重音落在 back 上，on 轻读 /ɑːn/，和 back 连成 ba-kon；past 的 t 碰到 month 只做口型，听起来像 pas month。
- I was **cook**ing **din**ner / when the **pow**er went‿**out**. —— was 弱读成 /wəz/，几乎听不见；前半句（背景）读得平稳，后半句（突发事件）went out 读重、语调往下落；went‿out 连读，t 直接带出 out，像 wen-tout。
- I've **just** **passed** my pro**ba**tion! / It was a **big** **mile**stone. —— I've 弱读成 /aɪv/，v 很轻但别丢；passed 读 /pæst/，和 past 同音；报喜的句子整体音调要高一点，milestone 重音在 mile。
- 第二册弱读总复习：have / has / been / was / were 在正常语速里几乎只剩一个轻音——I've /aɪv/、she's been /ʃiz bɪn/、was /wəz/、were /wər/。听的时候用规则判断：'s 后面跟 been 或过去分词（passed / seen）→ has；跟 -ing、形容词或名词 → is。used to / use to 读音一样，都是 /ˈjuːstə/（s 是清音，像 yoost-tuh），要靠前面的 am / is / get / didn't 分辨意思。
- 讲 2 分钟的故事，靠「路标词」带着听的人走：One night… / Then… / After the move… / But… / Last Sunday… / And…——每个路标词前停半秒，讲列举时语调略往上扬，表示「还有下一件」；最后一句语调落下，听的人就知道你讲完了。林晓的分享就是一个路标词一件事。

听力（四步听法：① 关键词听 → ② 不看文字听 → ③ 看英文听 → ④ 段落听写）。听辨要点：I've just passed 里很轻的 've：没有它就是另一个时态；I was cooking / we were climbing 里弱读的 was / were；used to /ˈjuːstə/：听到它先判断前面有没有 am / is / get
听写句：S8–S10、S20–S22
补充资源（可选）：在 youglish.com 输入 "look back on"，选 US，听美国人回顾一段经历时怎么用这个短语、后面跟什么。

## H 口语实战

**第 1 层**：合上书，不看【C】，按下面 10 个关键词提示，先用林晓的口吻（I…）讲她的两分钟分享，再用第三人称（Lily… She…）把整个英语角讲一遍，并录音：① 周六英语角 / 带 Leo / 话题「我的上个月」/ 回顾、先想一想 ② 第二个月 / 经历了不少事 ③ 做晚饭 → 停电（was …ing when…）④ 新房子走路就到 / Kevin 和吴佳帮忙搬家 ⑤ 以前每天地铁两小时 → 现在走路上班 ⑥ 搬家后发烧 / 一个人生病 / 慢慢习惯、更独立 ⑦ 上周日小梅去上海 / 从初中认识 / 很想她 ⑧ 刚转正！里程碑 / 感激团队 / 新篇章 ⑨ Leo 的反应 / Ben 问最难的部分 / 打电话 / 没以前那么紧张 ⑩ Joe 问有没有玩 / 凌晨三点 How come? / 日出 / 从没见过 / 值了 / Leo 很紧张 / 林晓和 Joe 的同一句话。第三人称版专门检查：She was cooking…、She has just passed…（不是 She have）、She's getting used to…、过去式有没有讲着讲着变回现在时。

**第 2 层 替换练习**

1. I was cooking dinner when the power went out.（替换：换背景和事件：taking a shower / the doorbell rang；waiting for the bus / it started to rain）

2. I used to spend two hours a day on the subway. Now I walk to work.（替换：换以前和现在：be really shy / talk to customers every day；drink tea / drink two cups of coffee a day）

3. I've just passed my probation! It was a big milestone for me.（替换：换成你自己的里程碑：moved into my own place / finished my first English book / run my first 10K）

4. I'm really thankful for my team.（替换：换对象，再补一句原因：my family / my mentor / my friends）

**第 3 层 自主表达**

1. 英语角上有人问 How long have you lived in your city?：你在这儿住了五年了，是五年前因为工作搬来的。（D31、D36）

2. 同事问 What were you doing when the typhoon came?：你正在家里看电影，突然停电了，结果你九点就睡了。（D38、D40、D42）

3. 新同事问 Are you used to the morning calls with Seattle?：说实话还没习惯；你以前起床特别困难，现在正在慢慢习惯。（D52、D53、D59）

4. 朋友说她换了一位新经理：你问「他是个什么样的人？」再问她和他处得好不好。（D56）

5. 同事说 I ran my first 10K yesterday!：你先表示惊讶，再追问一个细节，最后夸他一句。（D41）

6. 你要调去另一个城市工作，同事们给你送行：说你会很想大家、一定保持联系，你很感激这段时间大家的帮助，这是你的新篇章。（D58、D60）

**今日输出**：第二册录音测试（25 分钟里的前 13 分钟）。① 准备 5 分钟：纸上只写 5 组中文关键词，不写英文句子——上个月的一件事 / 一个变化 / 一个经历 / 让你感激的人 / 下个月的期待。② 录 2 分钟，中间不停、不重来：开场（When I look back on last month… / It was quite a journey.）→ 一件过去的事，用 was …ing when… 交代背景和突发事件，再说结果和感受（D38、D40、D43）→ 一个变化：I used to…, but now… / I'm getting used to…（D52、D53）→ 一个经历：I've… / I've never…（D32、D57）→ 感激谁、下个月的期待（I'm thankful for… / I'm looking forward to + -ing，D59）。③ 把 Day 30 的录音和今天的放在一起听，数一数：今天说了多长、几句？用了几个 when / so / but / then？was …ing when…、used to、have done 各用对了没有？有没有讲着讲着变回现在时？she / he 后面的 -s 和 has 呢？把结果写在「阶段成果评估」表旁边。说实话：两个月不会让任何人说得像母语者——你要找的是「比 Day 30 长、能讲一件有头有尾的事、时态基本稳定、敢接别人的话」。

## I 理解检测与当天复盘

1. （听力，不看原文回答）Ben 问 What's been the hardest part so far?，林晓怎么回答？她现在和以前比怎么样？

2. （听力，不看原文回答）Joe 听到「凌晨三点」，为什么说 How come?？林晓怎么解释的？她觉得值不值？

3. 改错并说明理由：I've passed my probation on Thursday.

4. 改错并说明理由：① I'm used to get up at six now. ② I use to live with three roommates.

5. 你网购的鞋太小了：给网店客服发消息，说明不合身、想换大一号；没货的话就退货。（D49）

6. 刚来的实习生问你怎么去公司附近的银行：直走，在红绿灯那儿左转，银行在超市对面。（D48、D50）

7. 用 for / since / ago 各说一句关于你学英语的话。

8. 朋友问 What's your sister like?，你回答 She's tall and has long hair.——哪里不对？应该怎么回答？

**阶段成果评估**

| 我能…… | 能做到 | 基本能 | 还不行 | 对应 Day |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 说清一件事持续了多久：How long have you…? 用 for / since 回答，分清 for / since / ago | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D31、D34、D36、D58 |
| 问和说经历：Have you ever…? — Yes, I have. / No, never. / I've never…，肯定句不乱加 ever | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D32、D57 |
| 用 just / already / yet 说「刚」「已经」「还没」的最新进展，分清 find out 和 know | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D33、D37 |
| 说「最近」和「变化」：What have you been up to lately? / Shenzhen has changed a lot. / Prices have gone up. | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D35、D36 |
| 讲一件过去的事的背景和突发事件：I was …ing when… / while… / during…，从头到尾用过去时 | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D38、D39、D43 |
| 讲一个有开头、经过、结果和感受的小故事，连续说 1–2 分钟（One day… / All of a sudden… / We ended up… / I was touched.） | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D40、D43、D44、D60 |
| 在对话中追问和回应：What happened next? / How come…? / No way! / Tell me more!，接住 6–8 个来回 | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D40、D41、D42 |
| 完成租房、搬家、看病的一次完整对话（How much is the rent? / Can you give me a hand? / I have a fever and a sore throat.） | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D45、D46、D47 |
| 完成银行开户、退换货、问路和指路的一次完整对话（I'd like to open an account. / It doesn't fit. / Get off at the third stop.） | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D48、D49、D50、D51 |
| 用 used to / be used to / get used to 讲过去的习惯和现在的变化，用 getting better and better / less … than before 说进步 | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D52、D53、D54 |
| 聊爱好、家人和朋友：What do you do for fun? / I'm into… / look like vs be like / get along with；送别时说 Let's keep in touch. / Good luck with… | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D55、D56、D58 |
| 听懂正常语速的对话大意，听出 have / has / been / was 的弱读；在长对话里回答开放式问题（感受 + 细节 + 例子 / To be honest… / I'm looking forward to + -ing） | ☐ | ☐ | ☐ | D34、D54、D59 |
**薄弱项目记录**

| 常见薄弱项 | 补强方法 |
|---|---|
| 现在完成时和一般过去时混用：有具体过去时间还用 have done（✗ I've passed it on Thursday. ✗ I've seen him yesterday.） | 开口前先问「我说没说具体时间？」——有 yesterday / on Thursday / last… / ago 就用过去式；重做 D32【E】第 1 组、D36【E】第 2 组、D57【E】第 4 组和今天【E】第 1 组 |
| for / since 用反；How long have you…? 回答时漏掉 for，或干脆不用完成时（✗ since two years ✗ I live here two years.） | 每天用 for 和 since 各说一句关于自己的话；重做 D31【E】第 1、2 组、D36【E】第 3 组、D58【E】第 4 组和今天【E】第 2 组 |
| used to / be used to 混淆（✗ I'm used to get up early. ✗ I use to be shy.） | 用 I used to… 和 Now I'm used to… 各造 3 句关于自己的句子，写下来检查 to 后面的词；重做 D52【E】第 1、2 组、D53【E】第 1、2 组和今天【E】第 4 组 |
| 讲故事时中途又变回现在时；was / were + -ing 漏掉 was（✗ I cooking when… ✗ Then she says…） | 复述【C】并录音，回放时专门圈出每一个动词；重做 D38【E】第 1、4 组、D39【E】第 1 组、D43【E】第 4 组和今天【E】第 3 组 |
| 第三人称 -s 和 has / have 仍会说错（✗ She have been… ✗ He don't…） | 用第三人称（Lily has… She's been… She doesn't…）复述 D59 和今天的【C】；重做第一册 D4【E】第 3 组、D36【E】第 1 组（Shenzhen has changed…） |
| 听力：正常语速里 have / has / been / was 被弱读后听不出来，分不清 she's = she is 还是 she has | 重做 D34【G】、D59【G】的段落听写和今天【G】的 2 段听写；每天听写一段含 I've / she's been 的句子；在 youglish.com 输入 "has been"，选 US |
| 问句问错：How come 后面又倒装；问性格却说 What does she look like? 或 ✗ How does she look like? | 每天说 2 个 How come…? 和 2 个 What's … like? 的问句；重做 D39【E】第 4 组、D42【E】第 1 组、D56【E】第 1、2 组和今天【E】第 5 组 |
| 对话接不住：别人问 How's everything going? 只回答 Fine，不追问、不回应 | 回答一律用「感受 + 细节 + 例子」，再反问一句；重做 D40【E】第 2 组、D41【E】第 3 组、D59【E】第 2 组，和朋友或自己对着录音练 6–8 个来回 |

**答案与示范**

- 热身：1. I've been to Beijing and Shanghai. I went to Beijing on a trip last year. 2. I've put on three kilos since I moved here. 3. While I was talking on the phone, I put it on the seat. 4. The jacket doesn't fit. It's too tight. 5. I'm not used to living alone yet. 6. I'm looking forward to taking on more responsibility. 
- 【E】小任务：1. ① I've just moved! ② I moved last Saturday. ③ Have you paid the rent yet? — Not yet. ④ I've never lived alone before. 2. ① I've worked at this company for two months. ② I've walked to work since I moved.（或 I've been walking to work since I moved.）③ Mei left for Shanghai last Sunday. ④ I moved to Shenzhen three years ago. 3. ① I was in a meeting when my mom called.（或 I was having a meeting when…）② We were climbing the mountain when the sun came out. 4. ① I used to be really shy. ② I'm not used to getting up early yet. ③ Don't worry. You'll get used to it.（或 You're gonna get used to it.） 5. ① What's he like? ② How come you didn't come to work yesterday? - 【H】第 2 层：1. I was waiting for the bus when it started to rain. 2. I used to be really shy. Now I talk to customers every day. 3. I've just moved into my own place! It was a big milestone for me. 4. I'm really thankful for my mentor. She always answers my questions. 
- 【H】第 3 层：1. I've lived here for five years. I moved here five years ago for a job. 2. I was watching a movie at home when the power went out. So I ended up going to bed at nine. 3. To be honest, I'm not used to them yet. I used to have a lot of trouble getting up, but I'm getting used to it. 4. Oh, you have a new manager? What's he like? Do you get along with him? 5. No way! How long did it take? That's really impressive! 6. I'm going to miss you all so much. Let's keep in touch! I'm really thankful for all your help. This is a new chapter for me. 
- 今日输出示范：Last month was really busy, but it was a good month. Let me tell you about one evening. I was working late at the office when it started to rain really hard. I didn't have an umbrella, and my phone battery was almost dead. Luckily, a coworker gave me a ride to the subway station. I was so grateful. There was also a big change in my life. I used to buy breakfast on the way to work every day. These days, I make breakfast at home. At first, I had trouble getting up early, but now I'm used to it, and I save about three hundred yuan a month. I've also tried something new. I've never been good at sports, but two weeks ago, I signed up for a badminton class. I've been to four classes so far. I'm still a beginner, but I really enjoy it. When I look back on this month, I'm thankful for my coworkers and my family. My English is getting better and better. To be honest, listening is still hard for me, especially when people talk fast. So next month, I'm going to listen to English for fifteen minutes every morning. I'm looking forward to the next chapter: Book 3!
- 【I】检测：1. 打电话（To be honest, phone calls.）；不过她没有以前那么紧张了（But I'm less nervous than before.）。 2. 他很吃惊，想知道为什么那么早（How come = 怎么会）。她们想在山顶看日出；林晓从没见过那么美的景色，腿疼了好几天，但觉得值了（it was worth it）。 3. I passed my probation on Thursday.（有具体时间 on Thursday，用过去式）；不提时间才说 I've just passed my probation! 4. ① I'm used to getting up at six now.（be used to 的 to 是介词，后面接 -ing）② I used to live with three roommates.（肯定句写 used to + 原形） 5. Hi, the shoes don't fit. They're too small. Can I exchange them for a larger size? If it's out of stock, I'd like to return them. 6. Go straight and turn left at the traffic light. The bank is across from the supermarket. 7. 例：I've been learning English for ten years. / I've practiced every day since I started this course. / I started this course two months ago.（for + 一段时间、since + 起点，配完成时；ago 配过去式） 8. What's she like? 问的是性格，不是长相；应该说 She's really easygoing and talkative.（D56）。问长相要说 What does she look like? 
**下阶段改善建议**

- 每天保持三件事：① 再忙也有 30 分钟保底（热身 10 + 朗读【C】10 + 【H】第 3 层 10），一天都不断；② 每天出声，至少 1 段录音按日期存档；③ 每周日用 2 分钟讲「我这一周」，至少用一次 was …ing when…、一次 used to / now、一次 have done。实话实说：两个月不会让任何人说得像母语者——第二册的目标是「能聊下去、能讲一件有头有尾的事」，时态偶尔出错很正常；第三册的目标是在工作中把事情说清楚、写清楚，靠的还是每天开口。
- 把薄弱项带进第三册的热身：从上面的「薄弱项目记录」里选 2–3 个你打了勾的（最常见的是完成时和过去时混用、for / since、used to / be used to、讲故事时态不一致），写在第三册 Day 61–66 的热身旁边，每天多说 1 句针对它的句子（比如：I've worked here for two months. I joined in…）；Day 67 复习日检查它还在不在。
- 第三册新增①职责与流程：林晓下个月要独立负责一组美国卖家——要说清 I'm responsible for…，用 first / then / after that 讲 5–8 步的流程；并开始学被动语态入门：The order is shipped. / It was delivered.（第一册的 It was delivered. 就是它的影子）。
- 第三册新增②汇报与客户沟通：说数字、百分比和趋势（increase by / to），看懂简单图表、写周报；回复客户咨询、解释规则、道歉与补救、礼貌地说「不」。第二册练的追问、回应和「感受 + 细节 + 例子」，会直接用在和客户、和西雅图同事的对话里。
- 第三册新增③系统与物流 + 书面表达：账号权限、报错描述、物流状态与异常，再写 6–10 句的工作邮件。第二册的时间表达不会丢：现在完成时、过去进行时、used to 会作为热身、复现词和复习日的易混检查继续出现；租房、看病、银行等生活场景不再从零讲，第三册以工作场景为主。

**复习安排**：明天——不看书说出 S3、S4、S5、S13、S14（look back on / reflect / journey / milestone / thankful / chapter）；把「薄弱项目记录」里打勾的 2–3 项抄到第三册 Day 61 的热身旁边。七天后——把 Day 1、Day 30、Day 60 的三段录音放在一起听，再录一次 2 分钟「我这一周」，比较长度、连接词和时态；再做一遍【E】5 组易混表达的小任务；还会错的那组，写进 Day 67 复习日的薄弱项。

# 附录 2 · 复习索引

| Day | 中英表达对比点 |
|---|---|
| D31 | 「我在深圳住了两年了」——I've lived here for two years. |
| D31 | 「两年」用 for，「从大学起」用 since |
| D31 | 「你来这儿多久了？」——How long have you been here? |
| D31 | 「我出生在湖南」——I was born in Hunan. |
| D32 | 「我吃过臭豆腐」——I've tried stinky tofu. |
| D32 | 「你吃过……吗？——没吃过。」——Have you ever…? — No, I haven't. |
| D32 | 「我从来没吃过」——I've never eaten it. |
| D32 | 「我曾经去过」——肯定句里不用 ever |
| D33 | 「她刚订婚」——She just got engaged. / We've just signed… |
| D33 | 「我已经给她发消息了」——I've already sent her a message. |
| D33 | 「张涛宣布了吗？——还没有。」——Has Tom announced it yet? — Not yet. |
| D33 | 「我刚知道」——I just found out.（不说 I just knew.） |
| D34 | 「我等了二十分钟了」——I've been waiting for twenty minutes.（还在等） |
| D34 | 「我学英语学了十年了」——I've been learning English for ten years. |
| D34 | 「好久」——for ages / for a long time |
| D34 | 「我手机没电了」——My phone died. / My battery is dead. |
| D35 | 「最近在忙什么？」——What have you been up to? |
| D35 | 「最近」——lately / recently / these days |
| D35 | 「照顾」take care of；「保重」Take care. |
| D35 | 「我想你了」——I miss you. |
| D36 | 「深圳变化很大」——Shenzhen has changed a lot. |
| D36 | 「房价涨了」——Prices have gone up. / went up last year |
| D36 | 「十年前」ten years ago vs「十年了」for ten years / since… |
| D36 | 「我胖了」——I've put on weight. |
| D37 | for / since / ago（复习 D31、D36） |
| D37 | 经历 vs 那一次：I've tried / I've been to vs I tried / I went（复习 D32，第一册 D10） |
| D37 | just / already / yet（复习 D33） |
| D37 | I've been waiting（还在等）vs I waited（等完了）（复习 D34） |
| D37 | lately vs these days（复习 D35） |
| D38 | 「我正在做饭的时候，停电了」——I was cooking when the power went out. |
| D38 | 「停电了」——The power went out. |
| D38 | 「我当时在洗澡」I was taking a shower. ≠ I took a shower. |
| D38 | 中文「正在」常省略，英文 was / were -ing 不能省 |
| D39 | while + 长动作 vs when + 短动作 |
| D39 | during + 名词 vs while + 句子 |
| D39 | 「我把包落在地铁上了」——I left my backpack on the subway. |
| D39 | 「它长什么样？」——What does it look like? |
| D40 | 「后来」——then / later / afterward 怎么选 |
| D40 | 「然后呢？」——What happened next? / And then? |
| D40 | 「结果……了」——We ended up -ing |
| D40 | 「总算 / 好不容易……」——I managed to… |
| D41 | 「真的假的？」——No way! / Really? / Seriously? |
| D41 | 「太厉害了」——That's amazing! / Way to go! |
| D41 | 表示感兴趣——重复关键词 + 追问 |
| D41 | 「好羡慕」——I'm so jealous! |
| D42 | 「怎么会……？」——How come + 陈述语序 |
| D42 | 「结果是……／原来……」——It turned out… |
| D42 | 「所以才……」——That's why… |
| D42 | 「以防万一」——just in case |
| D43 | 讲故事的开头——One day, … / One time, …（「有一次」） |
| D43 | 「突然」——suddenly / all of a sudden |
| D43 | 「我很感动」——I was touched. / I was moved. |
| D43 | 讲过去的故事，从头到尾用过去式 |
| D44 | 「我当时正在……」——was doing ≠ did（复习 D38、D43） |
| D44 | 「……的时候」——when + 短动作 vs while + 长动作（复习 D38、D39） |
| D44 | 「然后、后来」——then / … later / afterward（复习 D40） |
| D44 | 「怎么会……？」——How come + 陈述语序 vs Why + 倒装（复习 D42） |
| D44 | 「我很感动」「太丢人了」——touched / touching，embarrassed / embarrassing（复习 D43） |
| D45 | 「押一付三」——one month's deposit and three months' rent |
| D45 | 「包水电吗？」——Are utilities included? |
| D45 | 「我在找房子」——look for 还是 find？ |
| D45 | 「离公司近」——close to work / within walking distance |
| D46 | 「搬家」——move（不说 move house） |
| D46 | 「帮我搬一下」——Can you give me a hand? |
| D46 | 「装网」——set up the Wi-Fi / get the internet installed |
| D46 | 「去拿钥匙」——pick up the keys |
| D47 | 「我感冒了」——I have a cold. / I caught a cold. |
| D47 | 「吃药」——take medicine（不说 eat medicine） |
| D47 | 「看病」——see a doctor / go to the doctor |
| D47 | 「请病假」——take a sick day / call in sick |
| D48 | 「开户」——open an account |
| D48 | 「取钱 / 存钱」——take out / withdraw；put money in / deposit |
| D48 | 「转账、汇款」——transfer money / send money |
| D48 | 「工资」——salary / wage / pay |
| D49 | 「不合身」——It doesn't fit.（不说 It isn't suitable.） |
| D49 | 「退货」「换货」——return / send it back；exchange it for… |
| D49 | 「试穿」——try it on |
| D49 | 「缺货 / 没货了」——out of stock |
| D50 | 「坐错车了」——I took the wrong bus. |
| D50 | 「在……下车」——get off at…（不说 get down） |
| D50 | 指路——Go straight. / Turn left at the traffic light. / It's on your right. |
| D50 | 「在……对面」——across from… |
| D51 | 「找」：look for 还是 find？（复习 D45） |
| D51 | 「不合适」：fit 还是 suit？（复习 D49） |
| D51 | 「借」和「租」：borrow / lend / rent（复习第一册 D15 和 D45） |
| D51 | 「我冷」≠「我感冒了」；「吃药」take medicine（复习 D47） |
| D51 | 上车、下车：get on / get off 还是 get in / get out of？（复习 D50） |
| D52 | 「我以前在快递站工作」——I used to work at a courier station.（现在不了） |
| D52 | 「我以前不喝咖啡」——I didn't use to drink coffee.（拼写 use，不是 used） |
| D52 | 「以前」——used to / before / in the past 的区别 |
| D52 | 「我小时候」——when I was a kid（不说 when I was small） |
| D53 | used to do（以前常常）vs be used to doing（已经习惯） |
| D53 | 「慢慢习惯」——get used to（三兄弟一次分清） |
| D53 | 「我还不习惯」——I'm not used to it yet. |
| D53 | 「我想家」——I'm homesick. / I miss home. |
| D54 | 「越来越好」——getting better and better |
| D54 | 「客户越来越多」——More and more customers are… |
| D54 | 「我没以前那么紧张了」——less … than before |
| D54 | 「越练越容易」——The more…, the… |
| D55 | 「你平时喜欢干什么？」——What do you do for fun? |
| D55 | 「我喜欢做……」——enjoy -ing / be into |
| D55 | 「我不太擅长运动」——I'm not very good at… |
| D55 | 「报名」——sign up for，不是 sign in / apply for |
| D56 | 「长得像我爸，性格像我妈」——look like vs be like |
| D56 | 「她是个什么样的人？」——What's she like? |
| D56 | 「我跟我姐关系很好」——We get along really well. |
| D56 | 「我是独生女」——I'm an only child. |
| D57 | 「订酒店」——book a hotel，不说 order a hotel |
| D57 | 「办入住」「退房」——check in / check out |
| D57 | 「值了」「值得」——It was worth it. |
| D57 | 讲一次旅行：时态跟着内容走 |
| D58 | 「保持联系」——Let's keep in touch. |
| D58 | 「我会想你的」——I'm going to miss you. |
| D58 | 「祝你……顺利」——Good luck with… / I wish you all the best. |
| D58 | 「我们认识十年了」——We've known each other for ten years. |
| D59 | 开放式问题——How's everything going? / What's been the hardest part so far? |
| D59 | 回答不只说 Fine——感受 + 细节 + 例子 |
| D59 | 「说实话」——To be honest, … |
| D59 | 「我很期待」——I'm looking forward to it / to + -ing |
| D60 | 「了」「过」「刚」「已经」「还没」——have done vs did；just / already / yet（复习 D32、D33、D36、D57） |
| D60 | 「两年了」「从初中起」「两个月前」——for / since / ago（复习 D31、D34、D36、D58） |
| D60 | 「我正在做饭，停电了」——was doing vs did（复习 D38、D39、D43） |
| D60 | 「以前」「已经习惯」「慢慢习惯」——used to / be used to / get used to（复习 D52、D53） |
| D60 | 问对问题——look like / be like；How come / Why（复习 D39、D42、D56） |

| Day | 核心表达 | 什么时候用 |
|---|---|---|
| D31 | How long have you + 过去分词…? | 问对方某件事持续了多久 |
| D31 | I've + 过去分词 + for + 一段时间. | 说「……了多久了」，现在还在继续 |
| D31 | … since + 起点. | 说从什么时候开始、一直到现在 |
| D31 | I was born in…, but I grew up in… | 介绍出生地和长大的地方 |
| D31 | Where's your hometown? / Where did you grow up? | 问对方是哪里人 |
| D31 | I moved to … + 时间 + ago. | 说什么时候搬到现在的城市（过去的一个时间点） |
| D31 | I've lived here all my life. | 说自己一直住在某地（本地人） |
| D31 | We got to know each other in / at… | 说两个人在哪儿、什么时候认识的 |
| D32 | Have you ever + 过去分词…? | 问对方有没有过某种经历 |
| D32 | Yes, I have. I + 过去式 + 时间 / 地点. | 有过这种经历，再补一句「那一次」 |
| D32 | No, I haven't. / No, never. But… | 没有过，再补一句想不想试 |
| D32 | I've never + 过去分词 + before. | 说「我以前从来没……过」 |
| D32 | It smells…, but it tastes… | 说一样东西闻起来和吃起来怎么样 |
| D32 | It's my first time. | 说这是第一次做某事 |
| D32 | Give it a try! | 鼓励别人试一试 |
| D32 | I'm not a big fan of… / I can't stand… | 委婉地说不太喜欢 / 直接说特别讨厌 |
| D33 | Have you heard? + 新闻 | 引出一条新消息、好消息 |
| D33 | … just + 过去式. / … have just + 过去分词. | 说刚发生的事 |
| D33 | I've already + 过去分词. | 告诉对方「已经做了」 |
| D33 | Has / Have … yet? — Not yet. | 问一件大家在等的事做了没有；回答「还没」 |
| D33 | I just found out (this morning / from…). | 说刚刚得知一个消息 |
| D33 | Between you and me, … | 分享还没公开的消息 |
| D33 | Congratulations! / Good for you! | 回应喜事；肯定别人的决定和进步 |
| D33 | When's the wedding? / We're getting married… | 问和说婚期 |
| D34 | I've been + -ing + for / since… | 说一个动作一直进行到现在，还没结束 |
| D34 | How long have you been + -ing? | 问对方一件事做了多久 |
| D34 | Sorry, I'm running late. I'll be there in… | 还在路上，提前说会晚到，再说几点到 |
| D34 | My phone died, so I couldn't… | 解释为什么没接电话、没回消息 |
| D34 | Let's meet up at… | 约朋友碰头的时间和地点 |
| D34 | I've been waiting for ages! | 夸张地说等了好久（朋友之间） |
| D34 | Sorry, we've run out of… | 店里卖完了；东西用完了 |
| D34 | Thanks for being so patient. | 让别人等了之后表示感谢 |
| D35 | What have you been up to lately? | 很久没见的家人、朋友寒暄 |
| D35 | I've been really busy + at work / with… lately. | 回答近况：说一段时间以来的状态 |
| D35 | Not much. I've just been + -ing. | 没忙什么特别的事，但还是给一个细节 |
| D35 | 人 + has just + 过去分词. | 说身边刚发生的一件大事 |
| D35 | 人 + has been + -ing + every evening. | 说一个人最近一直在做的事 |
| D35 | Take care of yourself. | 叮嘱家人、朋友照顾好自己 |
| D35 | Take care. I miss you. | 视频、电话结尾告别 |
| D35 | Let's catch up soon. | 约很久没见的朋友聊聊 |
| D36 | 地方 / 人 + has changed a lot. | 说一个地方或一个人从过去到现在变了很多 |
| D36 | … years ago, this was… | 说一个地方过去的样子 |
| D36 | Prices have gone up / down. | 聊物价、房租、数量的变化 |
| D36 | How long have you lived here? — For… / Since… | 问和答「住了多久」 |
| D36 | I've put on / lost + 重量 + since… | 说自己的体重变化（只说自己） |
| D36 | Compared with…, … | 比较两个地方、两个时期 |
| D36 | Over the years, … | 说一个慢慢发生的长期变化 |
| D37 | How long have you + 过去分词…? — For… / Since… | 来自 D31：问和答「……多久了」，那件事现在还在继续 |
| D37 | Have you ever + 过去分词…? — Yes, I have. I + 过去式… | 来自 D32：问经历，回答后马上补一句「那一次」 |
| D37 | I just + 过去式. / 人 + has already + 过去分词. / … yet? — Not yet. | 来自 D33：说刚发生的事、已经办好的事和还没做的事 |
| D37 | I've been + -ing + for / since… | 来自 D34：说一件还在进行、一直在坚持的事 |
| D37 | What have you been up to lately? — I've been… lately. / These days, I… | 来自 D35：问近况，回答最近一直在做的事和如今的习惯 |
| D37 | 地方 + has changed a lot. / … years ago, this was… / Prices have gone up. | 来自 D36：说一个地方从过去到现在的变化 |
| D38 | I was + -ing + when + 一般过去时. | 讲过去的一件事：正在做 A 时，B 突然发生 |
| D38 | The power went out. / The power came back on. | 说停电和来电 |
| D38 | What were you doing when it happened? | 问对方「事情发生时你在干嘛」 |
| D38 | I was + -ing. What about you? | 回答「当时正在做什么」，再把问题抛回去 |
| D38 | Suddenly, … | 讲故事时引出意外 |
| D38 | I quickly + 过去时. | 讲你马上做出的反应 |
| D38 | Are you OK? / Is everything OK? | 邻居、同事出了状况，上前关心 |
| D39 | I left my + 东西 + on / at / in + 地点. | 说东西落在哪儿了 |
| D39 | While I was + -ing, I + 过去时. | 讲经过：在做 A 的过程中做了 B |
| D39 | What does it look like? | 问东西或人的外观 |
| D39 | It's a + 颜色 + 东西 + with… | 描述一样东西：颜色 + 东西 + 特点 |
| D39 | As soon as I…, I realized… | 说「一……就发现」 |
| D39 | Has anyone handed in a…? | 到失物招领处问有没有人交上来 |
| D39 | I'm so careless! I'll be more careful next time. | 自责粗心，说下次注意 |
| D40 | What happened next? | 听别人讲事情时追问后续 |
| D40 | So what did you do? | 追问对方当时怎么处理的 |
| D40 | First of all, … Then … | 按顺序讲自己做了什么 |
| D40 | Luckily, I managed to + 动作. | 说「还好，我总算做到了」 |

| D41 | Are you kidding? That's amazing! | 先表示惊讶、再夸 |
| D41 | 关键词? + How long did it take? | 重复关键词，再追问细节 |
| D41 | I can't believe + 句子. | 又惊又喜 |
| D41 | Tell me more! | 鼓励对方继续讲 |
| D41 | Way to go! | 夸别人做到了一件事 |
| D41 | I'm so jealous! | 开玩笑地说「好羡慕」 |
| D41 | My legs are so sore. | 说运动后浑身酸痛 |
| D42 | How come + 陈述句? | 问「怎么会……」，带着意外 |
| D42 | A typhoon is coming this weekend. | 说即将到来的坏天气 |
| D42 | The forecast says + 句子. | 转述天气预报 |
| D42 | It turned out (that) + 句子. | 说后来才知道的真相 |
| D42 | That's why + 结果. | 说完原因，引出结果 |
| D42 | It's been postponed, not canceled. | 说清是推迟，不是取消 |
| D42 | Let's stock up on + 东西. | 提议囤点东西 |
| D42 | …, just in case. | 说「以防万一」 |
| D43 | Let me tell you about + 某事. | 开始讲一件事，拿到话头 |
| D43 | One day, + 过去式句子. | 讲故事的开头 |
| D43 | All of a sudden, + 过去式. | 故事的转折、制造悬念 |
| D43 | I was really touched. | 说自己被打动 |
| D43 | The best part was + … | 讲最精彩、最开心的部分 |
| D43 | Thank you for your kindness. | 郑重地感谢别人的好意 |
| D43 | It reminds me of + 人 / 事. | 听完故事，说出自己的联想 |
| D44 | I was + -ing + when + 一般过去时. | 来自 D38：讲故事时先交代背景，再说突然发生的事 |
| D44 | While + 主语 + was / were + -ing, + 一般过去时. | 来自 D39：在一件事进行的过程中，做了什么或发生了什么 |
| D44 | What happened next? — … We ended up + -ing. | 来自 D40：追问后续；讲出意外的结果 |
| D44 | No way! / Seriously? / Tell me more! | 来自 D41：表示惊讶、鼓励对方多讲，再夸一句 |
| D44 | How come + 陈述句? — It turned out… | 来自 D42：问「怎么会」，再说后来才知道的真相 |
| D44 | All of a sudden, … I was really touched. | 来自 D43：讲故事的转折和感受 |
| D45 | I'm looking for + 房型 + near… | 跟中介说需求 |
| D45 | How much is the rent? | 问房租 |
| D45 | Are utilities included? | 问包不包水电等费用 |
| D45 | Is it furnished? / Is it noisy at night? | 看房时问房子本身的情况 |
| D45 | It's within walking distance of… | 说离某地很近 |
| D45 | … is a bit high for me. Can we negotiate? | 委婉地讲价 |
| D45 | The landlord wants one month's deposit and three months' rent. | 说明付款方式 |
| D45 | I'll take it. | 当场做决定：租了 / 买了 |

| D46 | Can you give me a hand? | 请人帮忙搬东西、搭把手 |
| D46 | Be careful, that box is fragile. | 提醒别人小心 |

| D46 | It's a mess. | 说房间、东西很乱 |
| D46 | Can you pass me the scissors? | 请身边的人递东西 |
| D46 | I've moved in! | 搬完家报平安 |

| D47 | Take this medicine three times a day. | 交代或复述用药方法 |

| D47 | I need to take a sick day. | 向经理请病假 |
| D47 | Take care, and feel better soon! | 别人生病时的回复 |

| D48 | I'd like to open an account. | 在银行说明来意 |

| D48 | Please fill out this form. | 柜员、前台请你填表；你也可以这样请别人填 |

| D49 | Can I try it on? | 问能不能试穿 |

| D49 | Sorry, … is out of stock. | 听懂或告诉别人缺货 |
| D49 | I changed my mind. | 改主意，说出新的决定 |

| D50 | It's across from… | 说「在……对面」 |

| D51 | Can you give me a hand? / … helped me set up … | 来自 D46：请人搭把手；说谁帮你弄好了什么 |

| D51 | It doesn't fit. Can I exchange it for…? | 来自 D49：尺码不对时换货、退货 |
| D51 | How do I get to…? — Get on … and get off at … | 来自 D50：问路；告诉别人坐哪路、在哪站下 |
| D52 | I used to + 动词原形. | 说以前的习惯、状态或工作（现在变了） |
| D52 | I didn't use to…, but these days… | 以前不、现在会 |
| D52 | Did you use to…? — Yes, I did. / No, I didn't. | 问别人以前的情况，并回答 |
| D52 | When I was a kid, … | 说小时候 |
| D52 | These days, … / Nowadays, … | 说现在（和以前对比） |

| D52 | I was the opposite. | 说「我正好相反」，把话题接过来 |
| D53 | I'm used to + 名词 / -ing. | 说「我已经习惯……了」 |
| D53 | I'm not used to … yet. | 说「还不习惯」 |

| D53 | At first, I had trouble + -ing. | 说一开始哪里做不好 |
| D53 | I feel homesick. / I miss home. | 说想家 |

| D54 | More and more + 名词 + are …-ing. | 说数量越来越多、趋势在变 |

| D55 | I enjoy + -ing. | 说自己喜欢做的事 |

# 附录 4 · 第三册衔接记录

- 课程天数：30；重点词 546 个 + 短语 135 个；对比点 124 组；核心表达 222 个；阅读量约 6739 词。

## 已学习的表达模式与中文思维点（第三册不再从零讲解，只在新语境中复现和加深）

- **「了」「过」「一直」**：have done（经历、结果）、have been doing（持续到现在）；for / since / How long…?（Day 31、32、34）
- **「刚」「已经」「还没」**：just / already / yet；find out ≠ know（Day 33）
- **「最近」「这些年」**：lately / recently / these days；ago vs for vs since；has changed / has gone up（Day 35、36）
- **「正在……的时候」**：was doing… when…；while vs during；「落下」left sth on…（Day 38、39）
- **讲故事和接话**：What happened next? / ended up / managed to / How come? / it turned out / No way! / Tell me more（Day 40–43）
- **生活场景**：look for vs find；fit vs suit；take medicine；open an account / take out money；get on / get off；across from（Day 45–50）
- **「以前」「习惯」「越来越」**：used to / didn't use to / be used to / get used to；getting better and better / more and more / the more… the more…（Day 52–54）
- **爱好与人物**：be into / enjoy -ing / be good at / sign up for；look like vs be like / get along with / take after（Day 55、56）
- **长对话**：How's everything going? / What's been the hardest part? / To be honest… / look forward to -ing（Day 58、59）

## 已完成的主要情境

- 聊各自在深圳生活多久、吃过什么、最近的新闻、和家人视频、城市的变化
- 停电、丢背包、机房漏水、台风取消活动等「一件事」的讲述与追问
- 租房看房、搬家、诊所看病、银行开户和汇款、网购退换、坐公交和问路
- 过去的习惯与现在的变化、业余爱好、介绍家人朋友、周末出游、送别朋友
- 和经理的试用期一对一谈话：被问开放式问题、说出困难和期待

## 需要在第三册继续复习的薄弱项目（多数学习者在测评中暴露的问题）

- 现在完成时和一般过去时混用：有具体过去时间（yesterday / last year / ago）还用 have done
- for / since 用反；How long have you…? 回答时漏掉 for
- used to / be used to 混淆（✗ I'm used to get up early）
- 讲故事时中途时态又变回现在时；was / were + -ing 漏掉 was
- 第三人称 -s 和 has / have 仍会说错（✗ She have been…）
- 听力：快速语流里 have / has / been 被弱读后听不出来

## 第三册应新学的内容

- **职责与流程**：be responsible for、first / then / after that 描述 5–8 步的流程；**被动语态入门**（The order is shipped. / It was delivered.）
- **汇报与数据**：数字、百分比、趋势（increase by / to）、简单图表、周报
- **客户沟通**：回复咨询、解释规则、道歉与补救、礼貌地说「不」
- **系统与物流**：账号权限、报错描述、API 与系统对接的基础词汇、物流状态与异常
- **书面表达**：6–10 句的工作邮件；**词汇**：约 520 个新词 + 140 个短语

## 第三册必须避免的不必要重复

- 第一册和第二册附录 1 中已注释的词和短语（第二册 681 个）**不再作为新词完整注释**，只在新语境中复现，并标注首次学习日
- 第二册已讲透的时间表达（现在完成时、过去进行时、used to）不再单独成课，只作为热身、复习日和易混检查
- 租房、看病、银行等生活场景不再重复，第三册以工作场景为主

# 附录 5 · 续写课程指令

```text
你是一名在中国长期生活、熟悉中美文化的资深英语教育专家和课程设计师。请按照《中文思维桥接英语 · 180 天课程》的既定体系，续写【第三册：Day 61–90 工作场景与基础商务沟通】。

一、必须沿用的体系（与第一、二册完全一致）
1. 每天 120 分钟，8 步：旧知识情境复习 10 → 中文意图到英语表达 15 → 微故事与词汇 25 → 语音 15 → 听力 15 → 开口 20 → 输出 12 → 复盘 8（Day 67 / 74 / 81 / 90 为阶段复习日，用复习日的 8 步结构）。每步写明具体操作。
2. 每课结构 A–I：A 今日目标（情境 / 关键表达 / 词汇范围 / 完成标准）；B 中文场景导入（人物关系、沟通目的、已知信息、表达重点）；C 原创英文微故事或对话，逐句配自然中文，句子编号 S1、S2…；D 按出现顺序的词汇注释（原文形式、原形、词性、美式 IPA、语境语义、使用场景、1–3 个常用搭配、英文例句 + 中文、中国学习者易错点、位置 S#）；E 3–4 组「中文怎么想 → 英文怎么说」（中文意思、自然英文、为什么不同、什么时候用、对比例句、小任务 + 答案）；F 5–8 个核心表达；G 语音与听力（重音、停顿、弱读、连读、缩读、四步听法、段落听写）；H 口语三层（模仿 → 替换 → 只给中文情境的自主表达，附示范）+ 今日输出；I 迁移型检测题 + 答案 + 完成清单 + 明日复习 + 七日后复习。
3. 每天约 20 个重点词 + 5 个短语，全部自然地出现在当天的故事、对话、邮件或聊天记录里；文本从约 220 词逐步增加到约 280 词。
4. 听力：先不看文字听 2 遍再答题，只对难句给中文；听写整段。
5. 复习日：用旧词写新情境故事（复现词标注首次学习日）、中文意思组织英文、短对话听力、不看原文复述、易混表达检查、下阶段改善建议。Day 90 提供阶段成果评估、薄弱项目记录和第四册衔接记录。

二、与前两册的衔接（附件为第一、二册的 Markdown 源文件，请先读两册的附录 1、2、4）
1. 两册附录 1 中已经注释过的词和短语不再作为新词完整注释，只在新语境中复现，并标注首次学习日（如 D35）。
2. 第二册的薄弱项目（完成时与过去时混用、for / since、used to / be used to、讲故事时态不一致、has / have、弱读听辨）要在第三册的热身、练习和复习日中有计划地反复出现。
3. 继续使用 SwiftLink 的人物：林晓已通过试用期，开始独立负责一组美国卖家；张涛、Emma、吴佳、李凯、西雅图的 Mike、实习生 Leo。

三、第三册重点
职责与流程（被动语态入门）、汇报与数据（数字、百分比、趋势）、客户沟通（解释规则、道歉与补救、礼貌拒绝）、系统与物流（账号权限、报错描述、API 对接基础词汇、物流状态与异常）、6–10 句的工作邮件；约 520 个新词 + 140 个短语。

四、质量要求
所有对话、邮件原创，不复制影视台词或付费教材；不做语法选择题合集；每个知识点都说明「在什么场景用、为什么这样说、中国人容易错在哪」；IPA 准确；不夸大学习效果；联网资源只给能核实的链接，无法核实的要说明。

五、交付
先给出第三册 30 天目录（每天的主题、场景、中英差异点、词汇范围），再逐天写完整教材，最后生成 A4 可打印 PDF 和可编辑源文件，并做完整性检查。
```
